## 🚧 V1 gaps — needed before onboarding a real school

Core to running a school's fees. Prioritized for the "no Monnify / no messaging needed" phase where possible.

- [ ] **Optional manual payment entry — per-school toggle, OFF by default** — The product deliberately does NOT build around cash. Core model: handle a school's full cash inflow **autonomously** via DVA/transfers, to avoid missing-money situations. A school may *request* to accept other forms (cash, POS); only then does the owner enable a per-account toggle **(via the admin dashboard)**, and only after the school signs a liability agreement acknowledging they accept and are responsible for non-transfer payments. *Depends on the owner/admin dashboard + per-school feature toggles.*
- [x] **Reporting & analytics page** — already built, see the dedicated section below (`/payments` analytics + `/reports` exports). The two remaining sub-items (fee/class drill-down, dashboard/RPC consolidation) shipped 2026-09-16.
- [ ] **Refunds / credits workflow** — currently only a "review for possible refund" warning; no actual refund path. **Deferred to v2** — not handled in this version.
- [ ] **Reconciliation UI** — surface the backend reconcile job; let a bursar review/match/resolve payments
- [x] **Reconcile cron covers Paystack schools** — **verified done 2026-09-14** (was stale — flagged as monnify-only). `src/app/api/admin/reconcile/route.ts` already selects `.in('payment_provider', ['monnify', 'paystack'])`, and `reconcileSchool` (`src/lib/payments/reconcile.ts`) is provider-agnostic — it resolves the provider via `getPaymentProviderForSchool` and calls `provider.listDVATransactions` + `provider.verifyTransaction`, both of which `PaystackProvider` implements (`paystack.ts:141,165`). So a Paystack school whose webhook fails/never arrives is picked up by the reconcile safety net the same as Monnify. No Paystack-specific path needed.
- [x] **Dashboard content for narrow-permission users** — a role with none of the widget-unlocking permissions (`see-financial-totals`, `approve-discounts`, `request-discounts`, `see-activity`) no longer lands on a blank page. If it has exactly one other reachable page, it's redirected straight there instead of landing on `/dashboard` at all (`getPermissionScopedNavItems`, `dashboard/page.tsx`) — chosen over a bespoke restricted-dashboard layout since a single real destination makes a more sensible landing page than an empty widget grid; may look scanty as a "home page" for some single-permission roles, revisit once final UI/UX pass happens. With zero or more than one reachable page, shows `NoWidgetsFallback` instead: a card grid of every page the role can reach, generated from the same nav config the sidebar uses (`src/lib/nav/navConfig.ts`, extracted out of `Sidebar.tsx` this pass) — so it never needs separate upkeep as pages/permissions change.
- [x] **Users & team management** — invite staff via emailed set-password link (`/settings/users`), reassign roles, deactivate/reactivate logins, resend invite. *(built alongside roles below; pending user test before ship)*
  - [x] **Sync sign-in time from Supabase** — fixed: `settings/users/page.tsx` reads `auth.users.last_sign_in_at` live via the service-role Admin API (`admin.auth.admin.getUserById`) instead of a stale `public.users.last_login_at` column.
  - [x] **Let Supabase send invites (drop SES dependency for now)** — switched `addStaff`/`resendInvite` to `inviteUserByEmail()`; Supabase now emails the invite itself. Still hitting `otp_expired` on the resulting set-password link for reasons not yet root-caused (redirect URL + Site URL both confirmed correct) — **deferred to the proper onboarding build**; unblocked testing in the meantime by provisioning test logins directly via the Auth Admin API.
  - [x] **Privilege escalation in Users & Roles** — re-audited: `addStaff`/`updateStaffRole` (`settings/users/actions.ts`) and `createRole`/`renameRole`/`deleteRole`/`saveRolePermissions` (`settings/roles-permissions/actions.ts`) already blocked a non-owner from assigning/reassigning anyone onto an `is_admin` role, and role CRUD/permission-editing was already fully owner-gated — vectors (1)/(2)/(3) from the original writeup were narrower than described. The one real gap: a non-owner `manage-team` holder could still assign a *non-admin* custom role carrying permissions they don't personally hold. Chose "cap delegation": `addStaff`/`updateStaffRole` now reject the assignment if any permission on the target role is off on the actor's own role (`ctx.permissions`), unless the actor is the owner. *(pending user test before ship)*
- [x] **Roles & permissions** — per-school custom roles with a 15-switch catalog (SEE/DO), editable at `/settings/roles-permissions`. Live `has_permission()` check (no stale JWT); enforcement across dashboard/fees money KPIs, analytics page, reports (page + export + money columns), sidebar, and all DO server actions. Owner/super_admin/is_admin bypass. All 6 migrations confirmed run 2026-09-24 (`roles_permissions.sql`, `seed_default_roles_trigger.sql`, `fix_bursar_permissions.sql`, `permission_based_rls.sql`, `loose_rls_fixes.sql`, `rls_privilege_fixes.sql` — the last one closes a self-escalation hole where `users` had no `WITH CHECK` on self-update). **Owner click-through test confirmed done 2026-09-25** (dev-browser walkthrough) — invite/custom-role/non-owner-edit-block/toggle-visibility/last-admin-lock/default-role-seeding all confirmed working. This was the sequencing gate on starting Paystack-integration work — now cleared.
- [x] **Discount revocation — unlocked by roles/permissions, confirmed 2026-09-24.** Discount revoke (`revokeDecidedDiscount`) is gated behind the same `approve-discounts` permission as grant approval, replacing the old blunt admin-only check. This is a direct, single-step revoke by anyone holding `approve-discounts` — not a separate two-person "request a revocation, a different person approves it" cycle (no such second step exists in the UI/actions). If a true two-party revocation-approval cycle is ever wanted, that's still new work; what shipped and is confirmed done is proper permission-gating of the existing direct revoke.
  - [x] **Dashboard "Pending Approvals" KPI fixed** — was unconditionally visible to everyone and sourced from the disconnected/unused `pending_approvals` table (nothing ever inserts into it, so it always read 0). Now sourced from the real `discounts` table and gated by permission: `approve-discounts` users see the school-wide pending count (links to `/discounts`); `request-discounts`-only users see their own pending-request count instead; users with neither see nothing.
  - [x] **Persistent discount-request status + duplicate prevention** — the invoice page's "submitted" message was local React state only (vanished on refresh, didn't stop other staff double-requesting). `getInvoiceByIdForSchool` now reads the most recent pending `discounts` row for the invoice; `InvoiceDetailLayout` shows who requested it and when, and disables "Request discount" while one is pending. `requestDiscount()` server action also blocks a second pending request on the same invoice.
  - [x] **Settings pages self-check (raw-URL bypass)** — the 5 "School" settings pages (profile, academic structure, payments, reminders, discounts) only had their *save* actions gated on `manage-settings`; the pages themselves had no guard, so a restricted user hitting the URL directly (bypassing the hidden sidebar link) could still view the config. Added the same `getAuthContext()`/`redirect` self-check used by `/reports/export` to all 5. Audit log (stub) gated the same way (bundled into `manage-settings`, not a separate key). Data & privacy (stub — will export/delete a whole school's data) is deliberately hardcoded to `ctx.isOwner`, not delegable via any toggle — a different risk class from the rest of Settings. Account security (own password change) intentionally stays ungated — it only ever touches the caller's own session.
  - [x] **Full button/page hiding for narrow-permission users** — closed the "user sees a button/link for something they don't have permission to do, then it fails on click" gap app-wide: page-level guards added to `/fees/structure`, `/fees/cycles`, `/fees/cycles/[id]`, `/fees/year-end`, `/students`, `/students/[id]`, `/invoices`, `/invoices/[id]`; `/settings`'s existing `manage-settings` redirect retargeted from `/dashboard` to `/settings/account-security` (previously bounced a non-settings user away from changing their own password); Sidebar/fees-hub nav cards and links hidden per-permission (Academic structure, Fee structure, Billing cycles, Year-end rollover, Invoices); mutation buttons hidden (not just disabled) in `StudentSettingsTab`, `SendReminderButton`, `HeaderVirtualAccount`'s create-account action, `ApplyDiscountButton` (separate `request-discounts`/`approve-discounts` gates), and `InvoiceDetailLayout`'s send/resend + request-discount actions. `record-payments` remains a defined-but-unenforced permission — flagged, not addressed here (no manual payment-recording UI exists anywhere yet to gate).
  - [x] **Permission dependency dead-ends fixed (no cascade graph needed)** — chose root-cause fixes over a generic dependency/cascade system, since auto-toggling permissions when the catalog grows would get unmanageable. `import-students` turned out not to be a real permission at all — bulk import was already gated on `manage-students` alone, so there was nothing to fix (the earlier `PERMISSION_DEPENDENCIES` claim on this line was fabricated, corrected 2026-09-02). `run-year-end` already works standalone with no dependency on `manage-fee-structure`/`manage-students` — a role scoped to just running the rollover isn't a dead end, so left as-is. The one real dead end: `approve-discounts` without `see-discounts` couldn't reach the approve/reject UI, since `/discounts` was gated on `see-discounts` only. Fixed by making the page, sidebar link, and nav gate all reachable by either permission (`discounts/page.tsx`, `Sidebar.tsx`'s `NavItem.perm` now accepts an array of "any of" keys); the server action was already correctly scoped to `approve-discounts` alone.
  - [x] **Dashboard "Dashboard" nav link hidden when redundant** — a role that gets redirected straight off `/dashboard` to its one other reachable page no longer keeps a separate "Dashboard" sidebar entry pointing at the same destination (`showsDashboardLink()` in `src/lib/nav/navConfig.ts`, wired into `Sidebar.tsx`).
  - [x] **Full permission sweep, second pass (2026-09-02)** — user-reported "Update invoice" button visible without `manage-invoices` led to a full re-audit of every page/tab/subtab (students, invoices, fees/cycles/year-end, discounts, reports, settings) via parallel agents cross-checking each UI control against its server action's actual `requirePermission` call. Found and fixed: (1) `StudentFeesTab.tsx`'s Generate/Update invoice buttons had no gate at all — now `useCan('manage-invoices')`; (2) same file's exemption/opt-in controls had no gate — now `useCan('manage-students')`; (3) `/api/invoices/[id]/pdf` had zero permission check, letting any signed-in staff member fetch any invoice by ID — now requires `see-invoices`; (4) `createDVAsForAllStudents` (the Payments-settings bulk-provision button) defaulted to requiring `manage-students` while its page/button are gated on `manage-payment-config` — aligned to `manage-payment-config`; (5) `SendReminderButton`'s "Send invoice" state (first send) was gated on `manage-students` instead of `manage-invoices`, letting a `manage-invoices`-less user text a parent an invoice notification — split so first-send now requires `manage-invoices` (both client `useCan` and a matching server-side check in `sendManualReminder`), while reminder/receipt nudges on an already-sent invoice stay under `manage-students` as documented. Also fixed a correctness bug found along the way: a stale (`needsResend`) invoice could still be sent via the reminder button with its outdated balance — now blocked outright (client shows inert "Needs resend" text, server rejects) regardless of who clicks it. All browser-tested and confirmed working by the user.
- [x] **Bank-transfer payment details on invoices** — shipped on both the invoice screen (`InvoiceDetailLayout`) and the PDF (`InvoicePDF`): shows the student's DVA account number + bank name, with "use the admission number as payment reference" and a "No virtual account yet" fallback when the student has no DVA. Gated on `invoice.dvaAccountNumber`.
- [x] **Notify parents when a paid/sent invoice silently changes (opt-in, fee edit, carry-forward, credit shift)** — **found 2026-09-14 while testing opt-ins + credit, core loop shipped same day.** The actual moment a sent/paid invoice's numbers change is when an admin clicks "Regenerate" (single, `StudentFeesTab`) or "Regenerate all" (bulk, `CycleDetailLayout`) — that's where the warn+prompt layer was added, since raw fee-item/opt-in edits don't touch invoices yet. `regenerateInvoice` and `startInvoiceRegenerationJob` now take a `confirmed` flag: with a sent-or-paid invoice (or, for bulk, a non-zero sent/paid count) and no confirmation, they return `{ needsConfirmation, current, updated }` (or counts) without writing anything; a `ConfirmDialog` shows the before/after total and credit change, and only a confirmed re-call persists (via the same atomic `apply_invoice_recompute` RPC used elsewhere — this also fixed `regenerateInvoice`'s old non-atomic 3-call undo/update/reapply-credit pattern). After a confirmed change leaves an invoice `needs_resend=true`, a manual "Notify parent of update" button appears next to the badge in both `StudentFeesTab` and `CycleDetailLayout` — calls a new `sendInvoiceUpdateNotice(invoiceId)` action (gated on `needs_resend`, reuses `sendInvoiceCore`) that sends an update-flavoured invoice message with an explicit credit-applied / new-credit-balance line (`composeInvoice.ts`). Nothing is ever auto-sent; the admin always clicks it. Pairs with the existing "Send all" bulk resend, which already picks up `needs_resend` invoices.
- [x] **Surface unsent invoice changes via in-app notifications + a non-blocking finalisation backstop** — **raised 2026-09-14, shipped 2026-09-14.** Kept non-compulsory throughout, per explicit owner direction: a receipt or any resend already carries an invoice's true current numbers, so nothing forces a school through an extra click. What shipped: (a) a **persistent dashboard KPI card** ("Invoices changed — not resent"), `getDashboardKPIs()` counting `needs_resend=true` invoices school-wide (not scoped to one cycle), gated on `manage-invoices` — since it's a live query rather than session/local state, it's visible to *every* admin with that permission and naturally reappears on next login for anyone, not just whoever caused the change; links to `/invoices?filter=needs_resend` which now reads that query param to pre-select the existing filter chip; (b) **`previewCloseTerm`** now also returns `unnotifiedChangedCount` (invoices in the term being closed that are `needs_resend=true`), shown in the close-term preview modal (`CyclesLayout.tsx`; relocated to the dedicated `/fees/close-term` page + `CloseTermLedger.tsx` by the Modernist redesign, same behaviour) as an informational amber panel with a **"Resend now"** convenience button (opens the existing `BulkSendInvoicesPanel` bulk-send job, then re-checks the count) — **"Confirm close"/"Close term" is never disabled by this**, closing proceeds regardless; (c) **audit trail as the actual backstop**: `closeTermAndCarryForward`'s `CloseCarryForwardSummary` (and therefore both its own `term.closed_carried_forward` audit event and `closeTerm`'s `term.closed` event, which logs that same summary as its metadata) now always includes `unnotifiedChangedCount` — recorded unconditionally, no confirm step required, so a term that closes with un-notified changes is on record either way. Year-end rollover's `continueYearEndRollover` calls `closeTermAndCarryForward` internally and inherits this for free. **Deferred, new sub-items below.**
- [ ] **Extend the unnotified-changes backstop to `out_of_date`/stale invoices and credit-balance-only changes** — split out of the item above. Today's backstop only covers `needs_resend=true` (an invoice that was regenerated but not resent). Not yet covered: `out_of_date`/stale invoices (a fee/opt-in changed but the invoice hasn't been regenerated yet — computed live per-cycle today, no persisted school-wide column, so a term-independent dashboard count would need one) and student-level **credit-balance-only** changes (no invoice `needs_resend` flag at all today — needs a new signal). Also note: `activateTerm`'s implicit close of the outgoing term (`fees/cycles/actions.ts:526-624`) doesn't go through the close-preview modal, so it doesn't show the informational panel (its audit event still gets `unnotifiedChangedCount` for free via the shared summary, only the UI nudge is missing there).
- [ ] **Design fork noted (pick deliberately):** the notify/backstop loop above keeps mid-term editability — the **chosen** approach. The stricter alternative, if ever wanted: lock an invoice once **sent or paid** and require an explicit "amend invoice" action (reason + forced parent re-send) instead of free editing. Owner chose the flexible+notify path 2026-09-14; recorded here only so the choice is visible, not to be built.
- [x] **Invoice overview should show gross + credit, not just net** — **found 2026-09-14, shipped same day; extended 2026-09-14; regressed by the Modernist redesign rewrite and re-shipped 2026-09-22.** `StudentFeesTab`'s "Current invoice" summary and `CycleDetailLayout`'s per-row Total both show the full breakdown when credit was applied — ₦70,000 − ₦20,000 credit = ₦50,000 — instead of a bare net total. The redesign's rewrite of the two remaining admin-facing surfaces dropped this display; re-added 2026-09-22: `InvoicesListLayout` (the global invoices list) shows a "− ₦X credit" note under the OUTSTANDING figure (the redesigned row has no separate Total column), and `InvoiceDetailLayout`'s Subtotal row now also shows when credit alone is the adjustment, completing the breakdown alongside its existing itemized "Credit balance applied" line (`getAllInvoices`/`getInvoiceByIdForSchool`/`getInvoicesByCycleId` in `lib/queries/fees.ts` already selected `subtotal`/`credit_applied` — this was a UI-only regression, not a query gap). **Still deferred, lower-traffic:** `StudentPaymentHistoryTab`, invoice PDF, and SMS/email first-send messages — those touch parent-facing documents/communications and warrant separate review.
- [x] **Block "Request discount" on a superseded closed-term invoice** — **found 2026-09-15, fixed same day.** Mirrors the resend guard (`b6fe6d1`): `requestDiscount` (`src/app/(app)/invoices/[id]/discountActions.ts`) now checks, when the invoice's billing cycle is `closed`, for a successor invoice via `previous_balance_from_invoice_id` — if one exists, the request is blocked server-side with an error pointing at the successor cycle, before the cancelled/paid/duplicate-pending checks even matter. `InvoiceDetailLayout.tsx`'s "Request discount" button gets a matching UI state (disabled, tooltip naming the successor cycle) using the same `carriedForwardToCycleName` field already loaded for the resend guard — no new query needed. A closed invoice with **no** successor (graduated/withdrawn student, the live record) stays requestable, same asymmetry as resend. Confirmed no other surface (`StudentFeesTab`, students overview) renders a "Request discount" control, so no other UI to gate.
- [x] **Opt-in/opt-out + send UX: toggle, confirm, toast — everywhere** — **decided 2026-09-15, shipped same day.** `StudentFeesTab`'s opt-in checkbox replaced with an on/off toggle switch. Opting **in** is gated behind a `ConfirmDialog` ("Add [Fee] ₦X to [Student]'s invoice? Increases what they owe by ₦X"), then shows a success `Toast`; the additive instant-apply engine is unchanged. Opting **out** stays instant (mark-stale → "Update invoice") but now shows a `Toast` pointing at the button ("Opted out of [Fee] — click 'Update invoice' to remove it from this invoice"). Added a `ConfirmDialog` before the single-invoice **Send/Resend/Receipt** button in `InvoiceDetailLayout.tsx` ("Send this invoice/receipt to the parent now?"). **Scoped down from "everywhere," owner-confirmed 2026-09-15:** bulk "Send all" already had its own confirm screen (`BulkSendInvoicesPanel`) and needed nothing; the per-row "Notify parent of update" button (`CycleDetailLayout`, `StudentFeesTab`) was deliberately left un-gated — one already-identified recipient, immediately after the admin just made the change themselves, no new-amount surprise — a confirm there would be pure friction for admins working through several students in a row.
- [x] **Opt-out on a PAID invoice: keep the paid record, defer to next term, let the school choose credit** — **decided 2026-09-15, shipped same day.** `toggleStudentOptIn` (`students/[id]/actions.ts`) now detects, on opt-out, whether the student has a paid invoice this cycle that would be clawed back (recompute < paid, same check `regenerateInvoice` uses). If so, the opt-in row is put back exactly as it was — **the paid invoice is never touched** — but flagged `carry_forward: false` (new column, `db/student_fee_adjustments_carry_forward.sql`) so `carryForwardFeeAdjustments` simply skips copying it to next term's cycle: the fee stops recurring for free, no extra bookkeeping needed. The admin is then shown a dialog (`StudentFeesTab.tsx`) naming the already-paid amount and offering **[Leave as-is]** or **[Credit ₦X to balance]** — the latter calls the existing `adjust_student_credit_balance` RPC via `applyCreditBalanceDelta`, same one `regenerateInvoice` uses. Cash refunds are explicitly out of scope, pointed at support/manual reconciliation (Scenario B copy). New action `resolveDeferredOptOutOverage` logs which choice was made either way, so there's always a record even for "leave as-is."
- [ ] **Deferred-opt-out UX gaps (found while browser-testing 2026-09-15, Test 5)** — the item above is verified working end-to-end (paid invoice untouched, `carry_forward=false`, ₦20k credit lands, audit logged), but the admin-facing UX has three gaps: (1) **no undo/cancel** — once the toggle is flipped on a paid invoice the defer is already applied and the overage dialog (`StudentFeesTab.tsx`) only offers Leave-as-is / Credit, so a mis-click can't be backed out; needs a "Keep [fee] — don't opt out" action that resets `carry_forward=true` on the existing opt-in row (no such action exists — `resolveDeferredOptOutOverage` only handles the money). (2) **misleading toggle state** — the opt-in row is deliberately kept for the paid record, so the toggle reads `isOptedIn=!!optIn` and still shows green ON even though the fee is stopping next term; `getStudentFees` doesn't expose `carry_forward`, so add a `stopsNextTerm` flag (opt_in exists AND `carry_forward=false`) to `StudentFeeItem` and render a distinct "Stops next term" toggle state instead of plain ON. (3) **confusing copy** — "Leave as-is" reads as "don't opt out at all" (owner-reported); reword to make clear BOTH buttons already opt out and the choice is only about the money: heading "[Fee] stops from next term. What about the ₦X already paid?", buttons "Leave it as paid" (subtext "used it this term — no credit") / "Credit ₦X to balance" (subtext "applies to future invoices"). **NB:** `db/student_fee_adjustments_carry_forward.sql` had not been run against the test DB — the opt-out-on-paid path errors on the reinsert until it's applied; run pending `db/*.sql` migrations before testing.
- [x] **Allow undoing a mistaken opt-in on a locked invoice when the added fee is unpaid** — **found 2026-09-15, fixed same day.** `regenerateInvoice` (`fees/cycles/actions.ts`) no longer hard-blocks on `sentAt || paidAmount > 0`; it computes the recomputed total first and only blocks when that total would drop below `paid_amount` (an actual clawback) — a sent-but-unpaid invoice, or a paid invoice safely reduced by undoing a mistaken opt-in, now regenerates and gets flagged `needs_resend`. Checked against the whole project for consistency: `StudentFeesTab.tsx`'s `isLocked` (single-invoice UI), `prepareInvoiceRegeneration`/`processInvoiceRegenerationChunk` (`invoiceGeneration.ts`, the bulk "Regenerate all" job, both the pre-filter and a re-guard against a payment landing mid-job), and `CycleDetailLayout.tsx`'s per-row "Locked" badge (now driven by a new `regenerationBlocked` field computed alongside `needsRegeneration` in `getCycleDetailById`, `lib/queries/fees.ts`) all updated to the same clawback-only condition. Removed the now-dead `wasOverpaid` return field and its handling — the case it covered is blocked before it could ever be returned. True clawback still errors, pointing at manual reconciliation (Scenario B).
- [ ] **Surface out-of-date invoices beyond the term-detail page** — **raised 2026-09-15, deferred same day.** A stale/out-of-date invoice (an opt-out or fee edit recorded but not yet applied via "Update invoice") is today ONLY surfaced on the cycle-detail page (its "N invoices are out of date" banner + "Out of date" filter + per-row badge). It's NOT on the invoices page, the students page, or the dashboard — so a teacher who opts a student out and forgets to Update invoice has no reminder (and a forgotten opt-out that then gets PAID can't be corrected downward that term — see Scenario B below). Mirror the `needs_resend` treatment: (a) a **login / pending-task notification** so an admin sees it on sign-in, (b) a **count/chip on the invoices page**, (c) a **per-student (or aggregate) indicator on the students page**, and (d) a **dashboard KPI** ("N invoices need updating"). Reuses the same staleness (`needsRegeneration`) the cycle-detail page already computes. **Owner decision 2026-09-15: hold off entirely.** The dashboard KPI card is the natural home for the login-notification piece, but the dashboard itself is getting redesigned as part of an upcoming whole-site redesign — building a KPI card into the current layout now would likely be thrown away. Revisit this item during that redesign, deciding then between (i) a live-recomputed KPI (cheap — only open/non-closed cycles ever drift, and a school typically has one open at a time — reusing the existing per-cycle `buildInvoiceComputePreload`/`computeInvoiceForStudent` staleness check) scoped to just the dashboard card, or (ii) the full build (KPI + invoices-page chip + a persisted `out_of_date`-style column with write-time invalidation at every opt-in/opt-out/fee-edit/exemption/discount call site + students-page indicator) if the redesign's information architecture calls for it elsewhere too. **When revisited (owner emphasis 2026-09-15): make it a visible BANNER, not just counts/chips/KPI cards** — the existing `needs_resend` surfacing is all easy-to-miss KPI numbers and filter chips; the owner wants a prominent warning strip (like the cycle-detail "N invoices are out of date" banner) on the pages an admin lands on — student detail ("this student's invoice is out of date — Update invoice"), invoices page, dashboard. Apply the same banner upgrade to `needs_resend` too (per-student "this invoice changed and hasn't been resent" strip), since today it's KPI/chip-only as well.
- [x] **Send a *receipt* (not a ₦0 invoice) on a fully-paid invoice** — **requested 2026-09-15, shipped same day.** New `sendReceiptCore` (`src/lib/invoicing/sendReceipt.ts`) reuses the same `composeFullPaymentSMS`/`composeFullPaymentEmail` + PDF-attach the auto-receipt-at-payment path (`applyPayment.ts`) already uses, so the manual and automatic receipts read identically. Wired as a new `sendReceipt` server action (`invoices/[id]/actions.ts`, `manage-invoices`-gated, logs `invoice.receipt_sent`). `InvoiceDetailLayout`'s send button now branches on `invoice.status === 'paid'`: fully paid → **"Send receipt"** (mint, calls `sendReceipt`); balance remaining → unchanged **"Send/Resend to parent"** flow. Manual/on-demand only, as intended — the auto-receipt at payment time is untouched.
- [ ] **Paid-then-corrected-down (Scenario B): manual reconciliation for now — no auto-refund** — **decided 2026-09-15.** When a parent pays an invoice that later should have been reduced (teacher procrastinated an opt-out), opt-out on a paid invoice is locked/deferred, so the reduction only helps next term. **Owner decision: do NOT build an auto-refund yet.** Handle manually via the admin/owner dashboard: an overpayment already becomes a **credit balance** (existing behaviour); an actual **refund** is done by the school contacting the owner to reconcile, until a self-serve refund system exists (refunds already deferred to v2). This is the core reason to push teachers to **resend the updated invoice to parents BEFORE they pay** — so parents pay against up-to-date numbers — which the out-of-date surfacing above is what enables.
- [x] **"Regenerate all" progress shows misleading "1/5"** — **noticed 2026-09-15, fixed same day.** `prepareInvoiceRegeneration` (`src/lib/invoicing/invoiceGeneration.ts`) now pre-filters the unlocked (not sent, not paid) invoices down to only the ones that are actually stale — running the same `computeInvoiceForStudent`/`buildInvoiceComputePreload` staleness check the cycle-detail page already runs for `needsRegeneration`, before the job is created. `total` is now the stale count, so `startInvoiceRegenerationJob`'s job `total` (`fees/cycles/actions.ts`) reads **1/1** instead of the misleading **1/5**. `lockedCount` (sent/paid, always skipped) is unchanged and still reported separately in the "N already sent/paid left untouched" note.
- [x] **Bulk "Send all" and automatic reminders excluded all closed terms — graduated-student closed-term debt was never swept** — **found 2026-09-15, fixed same day.** Confirmed both `startBulkSendInvoicesJob` (`.neq('billing_cycles.status','closed')`) and the reminder cron's `sendDueRemindersForSchool` (`src/lib/messaging/reminders.ts`, same filter) dropped every closed-cycle invoice outright — including a graduated/withdrawn student's **closed-term invoice with no successor**, which `b6fe6d1` deliberately keeps individually sendable since it's their only real record of what's owed. That debt had zero automatic coverage from either path, manual-send-only. **Decision: include no-successor closed invoices.** Both functions now fetch candidate invoices without the blanket closed-cycle exclusion, then look up which closed ones already have a successor via `previous_balance_from_invoice_id` and only drop those — a closed invoice with no successor now flows through bulk send and the reminder cron exactly like an open one. `InvoicesListLayout`'s "Send all (N)" count updated to match the same logic (reuses the `carriedForwardToCycleName` field already loaded for the resend guard, no new query).
- [x] **Exemption feature audited against the same gap classes as opt-in — no gaps found** — **checked 2026-09-15, after fixing the opt-in/discount/carry-forward bugs above.** `setStudentExemption`/`removeStudentExemption` were checked against: live-invoice reflection (correctly mirrors opt-out — next-generation only, `isInvoiceUpToDate`/`isLocked` go stale correctly), discount interaction (exempted fees are excluded from the discountable subtotal before discount computation even runs, so no stale-discount risk), term carry-forward (exemptions carry forward unconditionally and correctly — not gated by `is_recurring`, which the code already scopes to `opt_in` adjustments only, confirmed by design), and closed-term guard (already blocked via the same cycle-status check opt-in/opt-out use). One cosmetic nit, not fixed: exemption `notes` aren't preserved across term rollover (the exemption itself persists, just the reason text is dropped) — low value, not worth the churn.
- [x] **Roll-forward fee-item copy silently dropped `is_recurring` and `is_discountable`** — **found 2026-09-15 while confirming the recurring/one-time feature holds up under repeated term creation, fixed same day.** `createBillingCycle`'s "roll forward from" path (`fees/cycles/actions.ts` ~line 277-296) copied `class_id, name, amount, is_mandatory, is_optional_extra, display_order` from the source cycle's fee items into the new cycle — but not `is_recurring` or `is_discountable`. A fee item marked one-time (e.g. a uniform fee) or non-discountable would silently reset to the column defaults (recurring, discountable) the moment it was copied into a new term this way, quietly undoing an admin's earlier choice with no error or warning. Fixed by adding both columns to the select and the insert mapping. Confirmed via code read that this is the only place `fee_items` rows get created from a source cycle (year-end rollover only carries forward `student_fee_adjustments`, not fee items themselves), so no other copy site needed the same fix. Also confirmed a fee item's required/optional type can never be changed after creation (`updateFeeItem` never touches `is_mandatory`/`is_optional_extra`), so the invariant "only optional fees can be one-time" can't be broken by an edit either.
- [x] **Superseded the confirm-to-override regenerate flow with a direction-based split (2026-09-15)** — replaces the confirm/override mechanism from the "Notify parents..." item above. Reasoning worked through with the owner: adding an opt-in fee never needs to touch anything else on the invoice (no clawback risk), so it's now applied instantly and additively via `applyOptInAdditionToLiveInvoice` (`src/lib/invoicing/addOptInLine.ts`) regardless of sent/paid state — appends one line item, bumps subtotal/total/outstanding, logs `invoice.fee_added`. Opting out still only affects the next generation (unchanged). A **full regenerate** (touches discounts/credit/previous-balance/removals all at once) is the one action that genuinely can't be safely redone once a parent has acted on the invoice — `regenerateInvoice` (single) and `startInvoiceRegenerationJob`/`prepareInvoiceRegeneration` (bulk) now **hard-block** any sent-or-paid invoice outright instead of asking to confirm an override; bulk reports a `lockedCount`. UI: `CycleDetailLayout`'s per-row regenerate shows a "Locked" label/tooltip instead of the old confirm dialog; `StudentFeesTab`'s "Update invoice" button is disabled with a "Locked — applies next invoice" status in the same case. Opt-in-while-locked and opt-out-before-send-only (still fully editable pre-send) were both explicitly verified with the owner as the intended behavior.
- [x] **Recurring vs. one-time opt-in fees (2026-09-15)** — a fee like a uniform purchase should bill once and never resurface, while a club subscription should keep billing every term until the parent opts out. Added `fee_items.is_recurring` (boolean, default true; migration at `db/fee_items_recurring.sql` — **run manually in Supabase SQL editor**, no automated migration path in this project). Only meaningful for optional/opt-in fees — a "Recurring / One-time" toggle now appears in `FeeFormPanel.tsx` (single fee) and `EditFeeGroupPanel.tsx` (fee group) whenever the fee is optional, defaulting to Recurring. The actual enforcement point is `carryForwardFeeAdjustments` (`src/lib/fees/carryForwardAdjustments.ts`) — it now skips copying an `opt_in` adjustment into the new term's fee-item row when the source fee item is one-time, so the fee simply doesn't exist on the next term's invoice without any change needed to `computeInvoiceForStudent` itself. Wired through `addFeeItem`/`addOptionalFeeItem`/`updateFeeItem`/`editFeeGroup`/`getFeeGroupDetails` and the fees-structure list query.
- [x] **Withdrawn/graduated student's stray current-term invoice — admin decides, nothing auto-cancelled** — **raised 2026-09-15, shipped same day.** Gap: a student marked withdrawn/graduated mid-term after an invoice was already generated for them left that invoice dangling forever — no cleanup, no admin-facing way to cancel it anywhere in the app (the only prior writer of `invoices.status='cancelled'` was the automated rollover exit-cleanup for graduating students, `cycles/actions.ts:1761`). First pass auto-cancelled untouched invoices; **owner corrected this** — the school may still want the parent to finish paying for the term even after withdrawal, so cancellation must be the admin's per-invoice choice, not automatic. Shipped: `updateStudentStatus` (`students/[id]/actions.ts`) now only *detects* open invoices on the active cycle after a status change (splitting untouched — `paid_amount<=0 && credit_applied<=0` — from ones with money/credit already applied) and returns them, touching nothing; `StudentSettingsTab.tsx`'s confirm-status modal shows each open invoice with a "Cancel invoice" button (untouched) or a manual-review warning (money/credit applied); a new `cancelInvoice` server action (`invoices/[id]/actions.ts`, `manage-invoices`-gated, same untouched-only guard enforced server-side, logs `invoice.cancelled`) is also exposed standalone on `InvoiceDetailLayout.tsx` itself so cancelling isn't locked behind the withdrawal flow. **Cross-project bug found while checking consistency:** a cancelled invoice keeps its original `total_amount` (never zeroed), so several financial-total sums that didn't filter `status!=='cancelled'` were silently inflated by any cancelled invoice, including ones from the pre-existing rollover cleanup — fixed in `getFeesOverview`, `getAllCycles`, `getCycleDetailById` (`lib/queries/fees.ts`) and the dashboard KPI + `getCollectionByClass` (`lib/queries/dashboard.ts`); invoice **counts** deliberately still include cancelled invoices (real historical activity), only the expected/outstanding **sums** exclude them. **To test before marking done:** (1) On a student with an unpaid current-term invoice, mark them Withdrawn — confirm the confirmation modal shows that invoice with a "Cancel invoice" button, click it, confirm it disappears/shows "Cancelled" and the invoice's status flips to Cancelled on `/invoices/[id]`. (2) Do the same but on a student whose invoice already has a part-payment or credit applied — confirm it shows the manual-review warning instead of a Cancel button, and that clicking Cancel directly on that invoice's own page (`/invoices/[id]`) is correctly refused with an error (payment/credit must be resolved first). (3) On a normal, fully-open unpaid invoice (no withdrawal involved), open `/invoices/[id]` directly and confirm the standalone "Cancel invoice" button appears, works, and is gone once the invoice is paid or already cancelled. (4) After cancelling an invoice, check the dashboard KPIs, the fees overview, the cycle list, and that cycle's detail page — the cancelled invoice's amount should no longer count toward "expected"/"outstanding" anywhere, but it should still show up in invoice counts and in the cycle's invoice list/history. **Second cross-cutting audit, 2026-09-15, after shipping/pushing (`3a80400`) — per the standing "test for side effects, not just the feature" instruction, two agents audited cancellation against every other invoice-touching subsystem and found it wasn't actually treated as a terminal state everywhere. Bugs found and fixed same day:** (a) **resurrection bug (most serious)** — neither single-invoice `regenerateInvoice` nor bulk regenerate (`prepareInvoiceRegeneration`/`processInvoiceRegenerationChunk`) checked for `status==='cancelled'`, so a fee-structure change after cancelling could silently write a fresh total via `apply_invoice_recompute` and bring the invoice back to life with no admin intent behind it — fixed with a hard block in `regenerateInvoice` (`fees/cycles/actions.ts`) and a `.neq('status','cancelled')` filter at both the bulk-prepare query stage and the bulk-process write stage (defense in depth), plus `getCycleDetailById`'s staleness loop (`lib/queries/fees.ts`) now skips cancelled invoices entirely so they never even show as "needs regeneration." (b) **badge precedence** — `CycleDetailLayout.tsx`, `InvoiceDetailLayout.tsx`, and `InvoicesListLayout.tsx` all checked `needsResend` before `cancelled` in their status-badge logic, so a cancelled invoice that still carried a stale `needs_resend=true` flag displayed "needs resend" instead of "cancelled" — reordered all three so cancelled is checked first; also made `cancelInvoice` itself reset `needs_resend: false` at cancellation time as the root-cause fix. (c) **misleading UI on `StudentFeesTab`** — the student's fee tab had no cancelled handling at all; a cancelled invoice for the active cycle rendered as the live current invoice (mint-green "Invoice up to date" box), and since the DB has a unique `(student_id, billing_cycle_id)` constraint, `existingInvoice` stays pointed at the cancelled row forever — fixed by adding an explicit cancelled branch (distinct gray "Cancelled" box, "Update invoice"/"Generate invoice" buttons hidden, a "Cancelled — reissuing not yet supported" label instead). (d) **`InvoiceDetailLayout.tsx` still offered payment/send on a cancelled invoice** — the payment-instructions panel showed DVA "pay directly" instructions and the Send/Resend button rendered as clickable (server-side `sendInvoiceCore` already rejected it, but the UI was misleading); both now branch on `status==='cancelled'` first. (e) **dashboard/list counts inflated** — `getDashboardKPIs`'s `needsResendCount` and `InvoicesListLayout`'s `unpaid`/`needsResend` tab counts (and their matching filter predicates) didn't exclude cancelled invoices; all now do. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean after all fixes. New gap spun out separately below (can't reissue after cancelling, due to the unique constraint).
- [x] **Can't reissue a fresh invoice after cancelling one for the same student+cycle** — **found 2026-09-15 during the cancellation-feature audit, not yet built.** `invoices` has a DB-level unique constraint on `(student_id, billing_cycle_id)` — a student can only ever have one invoice row per term, ever. Cancelling doesn't free that slot: `prepareInvoiceGeneration`'s "already invoiced" check treats any row (cancelled or not) as "has an invoice, skip," and a raw insert attempt would hit the unique-constraint violation anyway. So today, once an admin cancels a student's term invoice (e.g. cancelled by mistake, or the student un-withdraws mid-term and needs a real bill again), there is no way to bill them again for that term through the UI — `StudentFeesTab` now correctly shows "Cancelled — reissuing not yet supported" instead of a broken/misleading button (see fix above), but the underlying capability doesn't exist. Needs a design decision before building: either (a) an "un-cancel"/restore flow that flips the row back to `pending` and lets it recompute normally, or (b) allow the generation path to reuse a cancelled row in place (update it back to a live invoice with fresh numbers) rather than inserting a new one. Not scoped or built yet — raised for review. **FIXED 2026-09-16:** built option (b) — see the reissue fix above (`regenerateCancelledInvoice` + `StudentFeesTab`'s "Generate new invoice" button). Confirmed by design a cancelled invoice can never carry a payment (that's `cancelInvoice`'s own precondition), so recomputing it in place is always financially clean — no refund/clawback bookkeeping is ever needed for this path.
- [x] **Mid-term class change — confirm before the move, then recompute the current-term invoice server-side** — **raised + built 2026-09-16 (rebuilt same day after owner feedback).** Gap the owner flagged: when an admin moves a student to another class mid-term, `fee_items` are per-class (`computeInvoiceForStudent` selects by `class_id`), so the fees that apply genuinely change, but nothing recomputed the student's existing invoice, nothing reconciled any payment already made, and the audit log only recorded a generic `student.updated` with no old→new class. First pass saved the class first and then showed a post-hoc "Done" notice, leaving the main page on the old invoice for the unpaid case; owner corrected it: the flow must ask the admin to continue or cancel *before* anything changes, actually recompute a clean invoice, and leave the page showing the new numbers. Shipped: `updateStudentDetails` (`students/[id]/actions.ts`) is now confirm-first via a `confirmClassChange` flag. On the first call, if the move affects the student's current-term invoice (at most one per term, `(student_id, billing_cycle_id)` is unique) it saves nothing and returns `needsConfirm` with a preview: for a **clean** invoice (no payment, no credit applied) it previews the projected new total by running `computeInvoiceForStudent` with a new `classIdOverride` argument (9th param, added to `computeInvoice.ts`) so the new class's fees are costed without any DB write; for an invoice that **has money on it** (payment or credit) it returns the paid/credit amounts so the dialog can warn that it needs manual review. On the confirmed call it saves the student, then for a clean+active invoice recomputes against the new class and applies it atomically via the `apply_invoice_recompute` RPC (records applied discounts, sets `needs_resend` if the invoice was already sent, adjusts credit balance by `-creditApplied`), reporting `invoiceOutcome: 'regenerated'`; a money-on-it invoice is left untouched with `invoiceOutcome: 'needs_review'`; no affected invoice returns `'none'`. Audit logs a distinct `student.class_changed` (new label in `auditLogLabels.ts`, maps to the Students module by prefix) with rich metadata (`oldClassId/oldClassName/newClassId/newClassName`, `affectedInvoiceId`, `invoiceOutcome`, `newInvoiceTotal`) and a summary noting either the recalculated total or that it was flagged for review; a pure name/admission edit still logs `student.updated`. `StudentSettingsTab.tsx`'s Edit-student modal now shows a real Cancel/Continue confirmation dialog before saving: the clean branch reads "₦{old} to ₦{new}", the money-on-it branch shows the paid/credit amounts and the review warning. On Continue the action reruns with the confirm flag; the page revalidates (`/students/[id]`, `/students`, and on a class change also `/fees/cycles`, `/invoices`, `/invoices/[invoiceId]`, `/fees/cycles/[cycleId]`) so the student page (a server component passing props to the client fees tab) reflects the recomputed invoice after refresh. A move that touches no current-term invoice saves with no dialog. The recompute runs for a `manage-students` admin as a direct traceable consequence of the authorized class change (not separately gated on `manage-invoices`). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. **To test:** (1) move a student with a clean unpaid current-term invoice to a different-fee class → confirm dialog shows ₦old→₦new with Cancel/Continue; Continue → invoice total and the student page update, `student.class_changed` logged with both class names and the new total; (2) same but on an invoice with a part-payment or credit → dialog shows the review warning, class changes but the invoice is left untouched and flagged for review; (3) move a student with no current-term invoice → saves with no confirm dialog, audit still records the class change; (4) Cancel on the dialog → nothing saved (neither the class nor any name/admission edit in the same submit).
- [ ] **"Why did my total change?" — money-affecting admin actions have no owner-facing summary** — **raised 2026-09-15, not yet built.** Owner's scenario: logs in expecting ₦12M outstanding, sees ₦11.5M instead, with no way to see *why* — which admin cancelled an invoice, withdrew a student, adjusted a credit balance, or approved a discount since they last checked. Confirmed via code read this is a real, total gap, not just missing UI: `logAuditEvent` has no financial/amount tagging at all; the audit log page (`auditLogGroups.ts`) has module-prefix filters (Staff, Invoices, Students, etc.) but no cross-cutting "financial impact" group; the Recent Activity feed (`activityMeta.ts`) only covers payments/invoice-sent/messages/discounts/student-added — it excludes invoice cancellations, student status changes, and credit-balance adjustments entirely; `credit_balance.adjusted` isn't even logged as an audit action anywhere (`adjust_student_credit_balance` RPC and its call sites have zero `logAuditEvent` wrapping). Candidate scoped fix, not yet confirmed with owner: add a "Financial impact" filter group to the existing audit log page covering `invoice.cancelled`, `student.status_changed`, `discount.approved`/`discount.recurring_revoked`, `invoice.regenerated`(`_bulk`), plus a new `credit_balance.adjusted` action added at the RPC call sites. A full before/after running-total ledger (so the owner sees the actual ₦ delta per event, not just that an event happened) would be a materially bigger, separate feature — scope that only if the filter-group version isn't enough.
- [x] **Adjustments made after a future term already exists never reach it — stale-future-term mis-billing (found 2026-09-15, browser-testing Test 8)** — **fixed 2026-09-25.** Built forward-fill propagation, not "always inherit latest" — that first design was rejected by the owner (correctly: it can't represent a non-monotonic pattern, e.g. opt in this term, opt out next term, opt back in the term after). Final design: `src/lib/fees/propagateAdjustment.ts` — `propagateAdjustmentForward()` runs right after an admin sets an opt-in or exemption, and fills the same choice into every future non-closed term's matching fee item (same name+class match as `carryForwardAdjustments.ts`, same `this_term_only`/`once_a_session` skip rules) **only where that term has no adjustment row of its own yet** — it never overwrites an explicit choice, whether admin-set or previously auto-filled. `retractPropagatedAdjustment()` runs on a true removal and deletes the same choice from future terms, but only rows flagged `auto_propagated = true` — an admin's own explicit row on a future term is never touched by either direction. New column: `auto_propagated boolean not null default false` on `student_fee_adjustments`, migration in `db/student_fee_adjustments_auto_propagated.sql` — **owner needs to run this in the Supabase SQL editor before the fix takes effect** (inserts without the column present will fail). Wired into all four adjustment-mutating actions in `src/app/(app)/students/[id]/actions.ts`: `toggleStudentOptIn` (forward-fill on opt-in insert, retract on true opt-out removal — NOT on the deferred-to-next-term paid-invoice path, which re-inserts rather than removes), `setStudentExemption` (forward-fill on insert), `removeStudentExemption` (retract on delete). `tsc` clean. **Not yet browser-tested — needs the migration run first, then a click-through per the owner's "test before commit" rule before this gets committed.** **Still out of scope, unchanged by this fix:** pre-planning a non-monotonic pattern ahead of time on a non-active future term isn't possible today regardless — there's no UI to view/edit a student's fees for a non-active term (`getStudentFees` hardcodes `status='active'`), which is the separately-tracked, already-deferred "view/manage fees for non-active terms" V2 item. This fix doesn't solve that and doesn't make it worse; an admin can still only correct each term once it becomes the active one, same as before.
- [ ] **View/manage a student's fees & invoices for non-active (past/future) terms — deferred to V2 (owner decision 2026-09-15)** — today `getStudentFees` (`src/lib/queries/students.ts:624`) hardcodes `status='active'`, so the student Fees tab only ever shows the active term; there's no way to inspect or set up a student's opt-ins/exemptions/invoice for a draft (future) or closed (past) term. Surfaced while testing Test 8 (had to confirm the roll-forward result via backend/invoice, not the Fees tab). **Owner: this belongs to a later whole-dashboard, term-switcher effort** (viewing the dashboard/students/invoices across past and future terms), not a one-off patch now — build it then. Pairs with the stale-future-term mis-billing item above (both are about draft/future terms being second-class today).
- [ ] **Fee-creation modal still needs a broader onboarding-friendliness pass** — noted while adding the Recurring/One-time toggle above: `FeeFormPanel.tsx` is already carrying required/optional, discountable, recurring, and scope (one/multiple/all-school) in one flat form, and a new school setting up fee structure for the first time will find this dense. Two ideas raised, not yet built: (a) a starter preset/fee-template list (Tuition, Bus, Feeding, Excursion, Uniform, etc. — **not** PTA levy, per owner: "not as common") that a school can select from and have per-school-copied (not shared/linked) rows created, renameable after; (b) letting the school just fill in amounts against recommended defaults rather than typing every field from scratch. Explicitly a V2/onboarding-flow candidate, not scoped for the current build pass.

## 📊 Reporting, analytics & data (a headline V1 feature)

The kind of useful information schools actually want to see and take away. **Distinction:**
*Reports* = files a school downloads (CSV/Excel — e.g. an audit/export they keep).
*Analytics/insights* = the in-app payments/insights views below.

- [~] **Payments / analytics page** — dedicated in-app view for financial insights *(built out: `/payments`, DB-side aggregation via `db/analytics_functions.sql`)*:
  - [x] **Client-side dashboard architecture** — one fetch of small per-cycle series (`getAnalyticsBundle`, 4 RPCs), then ALL scoping/aggregation/comparison happens in the browser (`src/lib/analytics/aggregate.ts`) with zero round-trips. Scales to unlimited history — selection is instant. Replaces the old scope-selector / searchParams / per-selection re-render model
  - [x] **Explore mode — stock-market timeline hero** — a full-history line chart (billed/collected/outstanding) with a drag-to-zoom **brush** + presets (This term / This session / Last 12 months / All). The brushed window drives every card below; hover any term for its figures (`TimelineHero`)
  - [x] **Period-over-period deltas** — KPI tiles show ▲/▼ % vs a smart baseline: a single-term selection compares to the same term a year earlier, a whole-session selection to the prior session
  - [x] **Compare mode — searchable multi-select (up to 5)** — type-to-filter picker over terms or sessions (handles 20+ periods), pick up to 5 → grouped billed-vs-collected bars, collection-rate bars, and a side-by-side table with ▲/▼ vs the first pick (`PeriodPicker`, `CompareBars`/`CompareRates`)
  - [x] **Compare overlay line** — alongside the bars: comparing whole sessions overlays each as a line vs term position (1st/2nd/3rd term) so years line up term-by-term ("compare years against themselves"); comparing individual terms is a single chronological line. Billed/Collected metric toggle (`CompareOverlay`). In compare mode the overlay sits above the bars, with the side-by-side table below
  - [x] **Fee price over time (the "fan")** — pick a fee → its price plotted per class across terms, so you can watch e.g. Tuition climb year on year with inflation. Handles fees that vary by class (a per-class fee fans into a line per class; a single school-wide price collapses to one "All classes" line). Needs `analytics_fee_class_series` (added to `db/analytics_functions.sql`); degrades to an empty state if not yet installed (`FeePriceChart`, `feeChoices`/`feePriceFan`)
  - [x] **Revenue by opt-in / fee** — per optional & required fee: students billed, billed, collected (est.), outstanding, rate
  - [x] **Outstanding / collection by class**
  - [x] **Discount waterfall** — Potential → −Discounts → Billed → −Outstanding → Collected, with foregone-to-discounts callout
  - [x] **Revenue mix** (share of collected by fee), **opt-in uptake** (adoption %), **collected-vs-outstanding per fee**
  - [x] **Discount impact by category** — chart + detail table (scoped)
  - [x] **Revenue by fee over time** — multi-line chart, one coloured line per fee by default (compare what each brings in); focus a single fee to see its billed vs collected
  - [x] **Drill-down from a fee/class to the underlying students** — **DONE 2026-09-16.** New `getClassDrilldown()`/`getFeeDrilldown()` (`src/lib/queries/analytics.ts`) query `invoices`/`students` directly (school-scoped, RLS-covered, no new RPC), exposed via `src/app/api/analytics/drilldown/route.ts` (gated on the existing `see-analytics` permission). Clicking a class or fee row on `PaymentsDashboard.tsx` opens `src/components/payments/DrilldownModal.tsx`, listing Student/Class/Owed/Paid/Status for that cycle set.
  - [x] **Seed data for testing** — `db/seed_analytics.sql` builds an isolated demo school (4 sessions × 3 terms + a summer coaching period, 40 students, invoices/payments/discounts) so the scope toggles can actually be exercised
  - [x] **Consolidate the dashboard onto the same RPCs** — **DONE 2026-09-16.** `getCollectionByClass()` (`src/lib/queries/dashboard.ts`) now calls the same `analytics_class_series` RPC the `/payments` page already used, deleting the duplicate JS aggregation (parallel invoice/payment fetch + per-class `.filter()`/`.reduce()`). **Real semantic change, noted per the design-handoff rule below:** "collected" moves from raw cash-received-by-date (from `payments`, could exceed 100% via cross-term prepayment/overpayment) to invoice-based `paid_amount + credit_applied` (capped at what's billed — anything past that rolls forward as credit rather than counting toward the current term). A class can no longer show over 100% collected on the dashboard. Consequence: `CollectionChart.tsx`'s "over 100%" UI (amber tooltip note, inline asterisks, footnotes, dynamic axis scaling past a variable max) became dead code and was removed in favor of a fixed `scaleMax = 120` — kept the logic layer free of an unreachable branch rather than leaving it for whoever wires the future redesign onto this data. If a future surface needs the old raw-cash "collected" figure again, it still exists in `payments`; this only changes what the dashboard's class breakdown means.
- [x] **Categorized activity / history page** — a browsable, filterable feed of the events we notify on (invoices sent/generated, payments received, receipts, reminders, messages, the discount lifecycle, students added), searchable by activity type and by student — so a school can find "what happened around X / this student." Rolling time-windows (last 30 days / 3 / 12 months + custom range) live here; analytics stays term/session-based. Built on a `security_invoker` Postgres view (`db/activity_feed_view.sql`) that unions the domain tables and resolves names at query time (never raw IDs) — deliberately NOT the audit_log, so system/parent-driven events (webhook payments, automated reminders) show too. Route: `src/app/(app)/activity/page.tsx`; query `src/lib/queries/activity.ts`; feed `src/components/activity/ActivityFeed.tsx` (colour-coded table by category); gated by new `see-activity` permission; dashboard "View full activity →" wired through. Demo data via `scripts/seed-activity.mjs`. *(Built & browser-tested.)*
- [x] **Downloadable reports / data export** — `/reports` page: a compact card per report (six exports — debtors/outstanding, collections, per-class summary, invoices, discounts, student directory), each with its own scope control (single term / whole session / all history; date range for collections; status for the directory). Every download is logged (who / what / scope / rows / when) and shown as a filterable "Download history" audit trail on the same page. UTF-8 BOM so Excel renders ₦ and accented names. Route: `src/app/(app)/reports/export/route.ts`; builders + history: `src/lib/reports/reports.ts`; audit table: `db/report_downloads.sql`. *(Reports page is also the future home for audit log + activity history.)* "It's their data — they can have it."
- [ ] **Incoming-payment feed** — chronological view of payments as they land
